HOW TO USE THIS DOCUMENT
------------------------
This is the authoritative build prompt for a local-first MPA (IndexedDB + Vite).
Implement EVERY screen below with identical behavior to the Express server app.
Each screen block defines: route, roles, permissions, UI fields, actions, workflows,
side-effects, and acceptance criteria. Do not skip any screen marked REQUIRED.

GLOBAL BUILD RULES (apply to every screen)
  G1. Repository adapter pattern — no direct IndexedDB in views
  G2. RBAC: route guard + field guard + service guard (triple layer)
  G3. billing.payments → ACCOUNTS only for paidAmount / collect payment
  G4. finance.close.submit (BM) vs finance.close.approve (Accounts), MC enforced
  G5. Workflow hooks fire in same transaction as parent mutation
  G6. Money stored as integer paise; display as INR ₹ with en-IN locale
  G7. Audit log on every CREATE/UPDATE/DELETE/approve/submit
  G8. Deep-linkable URLs; browser back button must not break state
  G9. Task queue must update after every workflow state change
  G10. PDF routes return application/pdf for bill, receipt, advance, challan, consent

│   GET /stock (warehouse.js) perms=warehouse.view
│ ACCEPTANCE:
│   1. Unauthorized role → 403 or hidden nav
│   2. Authorized role → page loads with correct data scope (branch filter)
│   3. Submit/action → persistence + workflow hooks + task queue refresh
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

################################################################################
# PART 6 — WORKFLOW ORCHESTRATION (EVERY HOOK)
################################################################################

HOOK: onAppointmentBooked
  Effect: appointment→CONFIRMED; complete NOT_JOINED/REGULAR/TREATMENT FUs; audit
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: onConsultationCompleted
  Effect: consultation→COMPLETED
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: onProcedureCompleted
  Effect: procedure→COMPLETED; appointment→COMPLETED; visit; slip; next session FU
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: onBillCreated
  Effect: link procedure.billId; complete PROCEDURE_COMPLETED FU; PENDING_ADVANCE FU if balance
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: notifyBillWorkflow
  Effect: onBillCreated + onPaymentRecorded for embedded payments
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: onPaymentRecorded
  Effect: if balance=0 complete PENDING_ADVANCE; scheduleRebookAfterPayment
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: onPharmacyBillCreated
  Effect: link procedure.pharmacyBillId
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: checkReorderAfterConsumption
  Effect: if stock<reorder create indent draft
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: onIndentApproved
  Effect: supply chain fulfillment prep
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

HOOK: onWorkOrderCompleted
  Effect: stock inward from WO
  Transaction: REQUIRED same tx as trigger

# PART 8 — EXTERNAL API STUBS (local-first)
################################################################################
POST /api/call-center/appointments — API key auth; creates appointment + onAppointmentBooked
GET  /api/call-center/slots — available slots by branch/consultant/date
GET  /api/call-center/customer/:phone — customer lookup
POST /api/online-orders — create online order; admin queue at /admin/online-orders
GET  /api/health — { status: ok, mode: local }
